     │ only): amount                                                           │
     │ = Σ unpaid of invoices with due_date ≤ today (MYT); nothing due ⇒ 422;  │
     │ 201 {                                                                   │
     │ billId, amountSen }; gateway_bills.id unguessable (bill_ + 122-bit      │
     │ random)                                                                 │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: ⛔ W2                                                               │
     │ Rule: /payments/return?bill_id=...&paid=true only displays the DB       │
     │ status,                                                                 │
     │ changes nothing (IF4)                                                   │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W3                                                                  │
     │ Rule: POST /api/webhooks/gateway, application/x-www-form-urlencoded;    │
     │ extra                                                                   │
     │ fields allowed and included in the signature                            │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W4                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Signature: all fields except x_signature, sort keys ascending by  │
     │ char                                                                    │
     │ code, key+value joined with |, HMAC-SHA256 hex lowercase with           │
     │ GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY, constant-time compare. Vector: key             │
     │ test_x_signature_key ⇒                                                  │
     │ 06994fab2d674e7829144eaa2204631716d3c099b503d38945e92b6296531641        │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W5                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Table: sig missing/wrong ⇒ 401 none; unknown bill ⇒ 404;          │
     │ paid=true &                                                             │
     │ paid_amount ≠ bill amount ⇒ 422; paid=true & pending/failed ⇒ 200, bill │
     │ paid                                                                    │
     │ + ONE approved online payment, received_at = paid_at, allocated;        │
     │ paid=true &                                                             │
     │ already paid ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & pending ⇒ 200, bill failed;      │
     │ paid=false                                                              │
     │  & failed ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & already paid ⇒ 200 no-op (paid      │
     │ never goes                                                              │
     │  back)                                                                  │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W6                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Duplicate/concurrent deliveries ⇒ at most one payment ever        │
     │ (UNIQUE                                                                 │
     │ payments.gateway_bill_id + bill row lock)                               │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │

     │ Rule: Never log the secret or whole payloads                            │

     │ Rule: node gateway-sim.mjs <bill_id> <amount_sen> [true|false] works    │
     │ end to end                                                              │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │                                                                         │

     │ - Do                                                                    │

     │      surface); leave signup default.                                    │
     │   b. Fix .gitignore (!.env.example). Write .env.example + .env.local    │
     │      (local URL, publishable key, secret key, DATABASE_URL=DIRECT_URL=p │
     │      ostgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres,            │
     │      GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY=test_x_signature_key) with a comment per   │
     │      var saying which supabase status field it comes from.              │
     │   c. Deps: npm i @supabase/supabase-js @supabase/ssr zod server-only;   │
     │      npm i -D vitest tsx.                                               │

     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 3. Key design decisions (recommendation, why)                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D1 Migrations vs Prisma. Migrations are the source of truth;            │
     │ schema.prisma is generated. Needed for RLS, grants, triggers, the       │
     │ exclusion constraint and the bucket, none of which Prisma Migrate       │
     │ models. Sync: npm run db:sync = supabase db reset && prisma db pull &&  │
     │ prisma generate, never hand-edit schema.prisma, commit it after every   │
     │ migration change. db pull cannot express the EXCLUDE/CHECKs; that is    │
     │ fine (they live in SQL), and I will verify the pull is clean in Phase   │
     │ 2. Prisma is used only for server-side data access (as owner, bypassing │
     │ RLS).                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D2 Seed. scripts/seed.ts (run by tsx --env-file=.env.local) calls the   │
     │ same service functions the API uses, with an Actor object, not HTTP.    │
     │ Users via supabase.auth.admin.createUser({ email_confirm: true,         │
     │ password }) with the secret key; memberships/tenants/units via Prisma;  │
     │ leases through createLease; payments through recordPayment (manager ⇒   │
     │ approved, staff ⇒ pending) then approvePayment, so invoices,            │
     │ allocations and audit rows are exactly what the app produces. Slip PNG  │
     │ uploaded with upsert: true (db reset does not clear storage volumes).   │
     │ Refuses to run if orgs already exist (prints "run npx supabase db       │
     │ reset"), so a double run cannot create duplicates.                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D3 Auth helper. One getActor(req) in src/lib/auth.ts. If an             │
     │ Authorization: Bearer header exists, validate it with getUser(token)    │
     │ using a stateless client built from the publishable key, and a bad      │
     │ token is 401 with no cookie fallback. Otherwise use the @supabase/ssr   │
     │ cookie client's getUser(). getUser over getClaims because it checks     │
     │ with the Auth server (sees revoked users) and the local Auth is fast.   │
     │ Then role lookup: memberships by user_id ⇒ staff/manager + orgId; else  │
     │ tenants by user_id ⇒ tenant + tenantId + orgId; valid login with        │
     │ neither ⇒ 403. Fixed pipeline in every handler: authenticate (401) →    │
     │ load the record through a visibility-scoped finder (404; malformed UUID │
     │ is also 404) → role check (403) → body validation (400) → state check   │
     │ (409) → cap/balance (422). The finder scopes by org_id for              │
     │ staff/manager and by tenant_id for tenants, so cross-org and            │
     │ cross-tenant are indistinguishable from "missing". route() wrapper maps │
     │ HttpError to {error} JSON. Server Actions and server components call    │
     │ the same getActor.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D4 Concurrency and overlap. Lock the lease row. Every money-changing    │
     │ operation (record+cap, approve, reject, void, webhook allocation) runs  │
     │ in one prisma.$transaction, first statement SELECT id FROM leases WHERE │
     │ id = $1 FOR UPDATE, then re-reads the payment/bill status inside the    │
     │ lock and guards the transition. The cap sum, the approve re-check,      │
     │ void, and allocation therefore all serialize per lease, and "approve    │
     │ twice" allocates once (second waits, sees approved, returns 409).       │
     │ Webhook lock order is bill → lease; nothing locks lease → bill, so no   │
     │ deadlock. Business logic stays in TypeScript services (explainable line │
     │ by line) instead of PL/pgSQL functions, which would also be new rpc     │
     │ surface to lock down. Overlap: EXCLUDE USING gist (unit_id WITH =,      │
     │ daterange(start_date, end_date, '[]') WITH &&): the database rejects    │
     │ the second concurrent insert (SQLSTATE 23P01 ⇒ 409), no app-level lock  │
     │ needed. Backstops: UNIQUE(payment_id, invoice_id) on allocations.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D5 Webhook idempotency. Row lock plus unique index. Order: parse raw    │
     │ body with URLSearchParams (duplicate keys ⇒ treated as bad signature) → │
     │ verify HMAC (constant time; lengths checked first) ⇒ 401 → read id →    │
     │ tx: SELECT ... FROM gateway_bills WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE (none ⇒ 404) → │
     │ paid=true: amount mismatch ⇒ 422; paid ⇒ 200 no-op; else lock lease,    │
     │ insert the approved online payment (gateway_bill_id, received_at =      │
     │ paid_at parsed with its offset), allocate, set bill paid, audit →       │
     │ paid=false: only pending → failed. A second concurrent delivery blocks  │
     │ on the bill lock, then sees paid and returns 200.                       │
     │ UNIQUE(payments.gateway_bill_id) is the structural guarantee ("at most  │
     │ one payment, ever"); a 23505 is treated as a replay (200), never 500.   │
     │ Logging: only bill id + outcome codes; no body, no key.                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D6 Data API lockdown. Deny by default, grant narrowly, helpers off the  │
     │ API surface. REVOKE ALL on tables and functions from PUBLIC, anon,      │
     │ authenticated, service_role, then GRANT SELECT to authenticated only on │
     │ the 9 readable tables, plus column-level UPDATE (phone) on tenants.     │
     │ Defaults are neutralised by revoking after creation in the same         │
     │ migration (plus ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES for the migration role).       │
     │ Policies TO authenticated, built on private.* SECURITY DEFINER helpers  │
     │ (avoids recursive RLS). Functions are not callable via /rest/v1/rpc     │
     │ because nothing lives in public and private is not in api.schemas;      │
     │ EXECUTE is revoked from PUBLIC as well. Tenant phone update = column    │
     │ grant + RLS user_id = auth.uid() (full_name/email/user_id/org_id        │
     │ updates fail with permission denied). audit_log: privileges revoked and │
     │ triggers on UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE that raise for everyone (so owner    │
     │ and service role are covered); INSERT only by Prisma/owner. Revoking    │
     │ from service_role too (Open Q7) because the brief says every other      │
     │ write must fail for everyone and the app uses the secret key only for   │
     │ Auth admin and Storage.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D7 Storage and cleanup. Bucket in a migration: private, 5 MB,           │
     │ jpeg/png/pdf. Policies: INSERT and SELECT for staff/managers whose org  │
     │ id equals the first path segment; no UPDATE/DELETE, no tenant or anon   │
     │ policy. Signed URLs (60 s) created with the user-scoped client, so the  │
     │ policy is a second enforcement layer. Upload happens first (client,     │
     │ user token), then POST .../payments carries slipPath. Server            │
     │ validation: path starts with <actor.orgId>/, no .., and a               │
     │ storage.objects row exists for the bucket (also re-checks size and      │
     │ mimetype). Cleanup: in the route's failure path, after the caller is    │
     │ authenticated as staff/manager, if slipPath is in the caller's own org  │
     │ folder and no payment references it, delete the object with the admin   │
     │ client. UNIQUE(slip_path) plus "only delete if unreferenced" means a    │
     │ failed request can never delete another payment's slip, and the loser   │
     │ of a concurrent same-slip race does not delete the winner's file.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D8 Time. Everything derives from three primitives in pure code:         │
     │ malaysiaDate(now) (billing.ts) for "today", parseOffsetInstant for      │
     │ incoming instants (rejects strings without an offset), SQL AT TIME ZONE │
     │ 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur' for month bucketing (report range = [first-of-from  │
     │ 00:00 MYT, first-of-(to+1) 00:00 MYT), zero months filled in TS from    │
     │ monthRange). UI: formatMyt is pure arithmetic with no Intl/locale, so   │
     │ server and client render identical strings; datetime-local text is      │
     │ combined with +08:00 rather than parsed by the browser; no new          │
     │ Date()/Date.now() in render paths. This also makes DECISIONS Q3 a short │
     │ list.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D9 Money in TS. DB bigint ⇒ Prisma BigInt; services do arithmetic in    │
     │ BigInt or in SQL (SUM); a single senToNumber() at the API boundary      │
     │ asserts Number.isSafeInteger. billing.ts keeps the contract's number    │
     │ fields (integers only) and uses BigInt for proration.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 4. Risks and fallbacks                                                  │

     │                                                                         │
     │ 5. Open questions (answer before we start; each has my default)         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Seed password for all 7 accounts (goes in .env-adjacent docs,        │
     │    README, submission). Needs your value.                               │
     │ 2. cacheComponents: true / partialPrefetching: true in next.config.ts:  │
     │    remove? Default: remove both (every page here is per-user dynamic;   │
     │    flags add Suspense/prerender rules and build-error risk; the brief   │
     │    does not need them). Keep the Tailwind turbopack loader.             │
     │ 3. Branch/remote: rename master → main? Who creates the public GitHub   │
     │    repo and what is the remote URL? Default: rename to main; I never    │
     │    push until you say so.                                               │
     │ 4. Webhook gaps (brief is silent): (a) paid=true but transaction_status │
     │    is failed/pending: default: ignore transaction_status, trust paid.   │
     │    (b) paid=true with wrong paid_amount on an already-paid bill:        │
     │    default: 422 (amount check before status). (c) missing/unparseable   │
     │    paid_at or non-numeric paid_amount with a valid signature: default:  │
     │    400.                                                                 │
     │ 5. Void of an online payment that still has unapplied_sen (DECISIONS    │
     │    Q5): default: do nothing to other payments (brief: their allocations │
     │    do not change), leave unapplied credit as is, and leave the voided   │
     │    payment's own unapplied_sen untouched. Alternative: zero it on void  │
     │    or auto-reallocate. Your call.                                       │
     │ 6. Manager-recorded payment (approved immediately): write an audit_log  │
     │    row payment.approved too? Default: yes (details mark auto: true).    │
     │ 7. service_role on public tables via Data API: default: revoke          │
     │    everything ("every other insert/update/delete must fail for          │
     │    everyone"). Risk: a hidden test that inserts an audit row with the   │
     │    service key as setup would fail.                                     │
     │ 8. /payments/return: require login and show "not found" unless the bill │
     │    belongs to the viewer (tenant of the lease, or staff/manager of its  │
     │    org)? Default: yes. Alternative: public by unguessable bill id,      │
     │    status only.                                                         │
     │ 9. Prisma version: default: 7.10.1 for CLI, client and adapter (not the │
     │    8.0.0-rc.28 that latest resolves to).                                │
     │ 10. Defaults I will apply unless you object: cross-org or unknown       │
     │     unitId/tenantId/leaseId in a body or path ⇒ 404 (CLAUDE.md rule);   │
     │     reason is trimmed before the 5-char test; cash with a slipPath ⇒    │
     │     400; receivedAt without an offset ⇒ 400; amountSen must be a        │
     │     positive safe integer ⇒ 400; valid login with no membership/tenant  │
     │     row ⇒ 403; collections range has no maximum span, from > to or bad  │
     │     format ⇒ 400; invalid UUID in a path ⇒ 404.                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Appendix A: REVIEW.md prep (Part B), to be verified against the         │
     │ snippet's line numbers in Phase 7                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Ordered most → least severe. Only real problems; "looks wrong but fine" │
     │ listed separately.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ late-fees/route.ts                                                      │
     │ 1. Critical L401: role read from user_metadata (user-editable) ⇒ any    │
     │    user escalates to manager. Fix: role from memberships.               │
     │ 2. Critical L406: no org/ownership check on leaseId; Prisma bypasses    │
     │    RLS ⇒ a manager of org B adds fees to org A's lease (IDOR). Fix:     │
     │    scope lookup by actor's org ⇒ 404.                                   │
     │ 3. High L415–434: not idempotent (second call adds the same fees        │
     │    again), no transaction (partial writes on failure), no lease lock    │
     │    (concurrent calls duplicate).                                        │
     │ 4. High L429/L438–440: reference: await nextReference() is resolved     │
     │    before any create runs (Prisma queries are lazy), so every invoice   │
     │    in the loop gets the same INV-n; also read-then-write race and       │
     │    parseInt of a null/other-format reference gives INV-NaN.             │
     │ 5. Medium L421: kind: 'late_fee' is outside the allowed invoice kinds   │
     │    (deposit/rent) and breaks the lease balance/cap model.               │
     │ 6. Medium L419–420: money via floats (Number(...)/Number(...) >= 0.995  │
     │    silently forgives up to 0.5%; Math.round(x*2/100)). Fix: BigInt      │
     │    integer math, explicit rule.                                         │
     │ 7. Low L401–403: unauthenticated returns 403, should be 401; L427 new   │
     │    Date(todayMyt) fine (UTC midnight) but only works because Prisma     │
     │    reads DATE as UTC.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ webhooks/gateway/route.ts                                               │
     │ 1. Critical L458: logs fields and the expected signature ⇒ anyone with  │
     │    log access can forge valid callbacks (and logs whole payload,        │
     │    forbidden).                                                          │
     │ 2. High L463–475: read-then-write race, no lock; two concurrent         │
     │    deliveries both see pending ⇒ double payment (or 500 on unique).     │
     │    Fix: FOR UPDATE on bill row in the transaction + unique              │
     │    gateway_bill_id.                                                     │
     │ 3. High L461: paid !== 'true' returns 200 before the bill lookup ⇒      │
     │    unknown id never 404, pending bill never becomes failed.             │
     │ 4. High L475: BigInt(fields.paid_amount) throws on junk (500) and is    │
     │    never compared to bill.amountSen ⇒ attacker or gateway bug settles a │
     │    bill with any amount (422 required).                                 │
     │ 5. Medium L476: receivedAt: new Date() instead of paid_at ⇒ wrong month │
     │    in collections.                                                      │
     │ 6. Medium L457: !== string compare is not constant-time; ! on a missing │
     │    env var gives a 500.                                                 │
     │ 7. Medium L467–481: allocation without locking the lease ⇒ races with   │
     │    approvals/other allocations.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ dashboard/actions.ts                                                    │
     │ 1. Critical L490: 'use server' export is a public endpoint; orgId comes │
     │    from the client, no authentication/authorization ⇒ any caller        │
     │    (including tenants/anon) reads any org's collections.                │
     │ 2. High L492–495: date_trunc('month', received_at) and ::date           │
     │    comparisons use the DB session timezone (UTC) ⇒ payments between     │
     │    00:00–08:00 MYT on the 1st land in the previous month, range edges   │
     │    shifted 8 h. Fix: AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur' on both.          │
     │ 3. Medium L497: months with no payments are omitted (brief requires     │
     │    zero rows); no validation of from/to (bad input ⇒ 500).              │
     │ 4. Looks suspicious but fine: the tagged-template $queryRaw is          │
     │    parameterised (no SQL injection); Number(r.total) is safe within the │
     │    safe-integer range (type annotation bigint is wrong, since SUM       │
     │    returns numeric, but the conversion still works).                    │

     │ Phase: 4                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: P10                                                                 │
     │ Rule: GET /api/reports/collections?from=YYYY-MM&to=YYYY-MM: Σ approved  │
     │ payments                                                                │
     │ per month of received_at in MYT, every month in range incl. zeros,      │
     │ ordered.                                                                │
     │ Seed: Northwind 2026-01..03 =                                           │
     │ [{2026-01,300000},{2026-02,0},{2026-03,200000}]                         │
     │  (the 2026-03-01 00:30 MYT payment is 2026-02-28T16:30Z, the trap)      │
     │ Phase: 4                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ W: Online payments & webhook (15 pts)                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ID: W1                                                                  │
     │ Rule: POST /api/leases/:leaseId/gateway-bills (the lease's tenant       │
     │ only): amount                                                           │
     │ = Σ unpaid of invoices with due_date ≤ today (MYT); nothing due ⇒ 422;  │
     │ 201 {                                                                   │
     │ billId, amountSen }; gateway_bills.id unguessable (bill_ + 122-bit      │
     │ random)                                                                 │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: ⛔ W2                                                               │
     │ Rule: /payments/return?bill_id=...&paid=true only displays the DB       │
     │ status,                                                                 │
     │ changes nothing (IF4)                                                   │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │

     │ Rule: POST /api/webhooks/gateway, application/x-www-form-urlencoded;    │
     │ extra                                                                   │
     │ fields allowed and included in the signature                            │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W4                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Signature: all fields except x_signature, sort keys ascending by  │
     │ char                                                                    │
     │ code, key+value joined with |, HMAC-SHA256 hex lowercase with           │
     │ GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY, constant-time compare. Vector: key             │
     │ test_x_signature_key ⇒                                                  │
     │ 06994fab2d674e7829144eaa2204631716d3c099b503d38945e92b6296531641        │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W5                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Table: sig missing/wrong ⇒ 401 none; unknown bill ⇒ 404;          │
     │ paid=true &                                                             │
     │ paid_amount ≠ bill amount ⇒ 422; paid=true & pending/failed ⇒ 200, bill │
     │ paid                                                                    │
     │ + ONE approved online payment, received_at = paid_at, allocated;        │
     │ paid=true &                                                             │
     │ already paid ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & pending ⇒ 200, bill failed;      │
     │ paid=false                                                              │
     │  & failed ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & already paid ⇒ 200 no-op (paid      │
     │ never goes                                                              │
     │  back)                                                                  │
     │ Phase: 5                                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ ID: W6                                                                  │
     │ Rule: Duplicate/concurrent deliveries ⇒ at most one payment ever        │

     │ Phase: 5                                                                │

     │ /auth/v1/token?grant_type=password), used as -H "Authorization: Bearer  │
     │ $T" in curl.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │

     │ - Migrations (npx supabase migration new ..., applied in order):        │
     │   a. *_schema.sql: btree_gist; enums (invoice_kind, payment_method,     │
     │      payment_status, bill_status, member_role); the 10 tables with uuid │
     │      default gen_random_uuid() PKs (gateway_bills.id = 'bill_' ||       │
     │      replace(gen_random_uuid()::text,'-','')); FKs; CHECKs (amounts,    │
     │      end_date >= start_date, unapplied_sen between 0 and amount_sen,    │
     │      rent > 0, deposit >= 0); UNIQUE(memberships.user_id);              │
     │      UNIQUE(tenants.user_id); UNIQUE(payments.gateway_bill_id);         │
     │      UNIQUE(payments.slip_path) where not null; one deposit per lease,  │
     │      UNIQUE(lease_id, period_index); UNIQUE(payment_id, invoice_id) on  │
     │      allocations; composite FKs leases(unit_id,org_id)→units(id,org_id) │
     │      and leases(tenant_id,org_id)→tenants(id,org_id); EXCLUDE USING     │
     │      gist (unit_id WITH =, daterange(start_date, end_date, '[]') WITH   │
     │      &&); indexes on lease_id/org_id. Extra columns of ours:            │
     │      payments.created_by/decided_by/decided_at/decision_reason,         │
     │      created_ats, audit_log.actor_id nullable (system actions).         │
     │   b. *_audit_append_only.sql: row trigger BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE +     │
     │      statement trigger BEFORE TRUNCATE raising for every role incl.     │
     │      service_role and the owner (D10); additionally revoke              │
     │      UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE from anon/authenticated only.               │
     │   c. *_security.sql: schema private (not in api.schemas) holding        │
     │      SECURITY DEFINER helpers with set search_path = ''                 │
     │      (staff_org_ids(), manager_org_ids(), my_tenant_ids(),              │
     │      my_lease_ids()); ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY on all 10 tables;       │
     │      REVOKE ALL ... FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated on public tables   │
     │      (not service_role, per Q7) and REVOKE EXECUTE ... FROM PUBLIC,     │
     │      anon on functions; GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA private + GRANT EXECUTE   │
     │      on the private.* helpers TO authenticated (required for RLS to     │
     │      evaluate); then GRANT SELECT to authenticated per D1–D6 and GRANT  │
     │      UPDATE (phone) ON tenants + RLS update policy user_id =            │
     │      auth.uid(); policies are TO authenticated only; no write policies  │
     │      anywhere else.                                                     │
     │   d. *_storage.sql: bucket payment-slips (private, file_size_limit      │
     │      5242880, allowed_mime_types jpeg/png/pdf); storage.objects         │
     │      policies for authenticated: INSERT and SELECT only when            │
     │      bucket_id='payment-slips' and the first folder segment equals an   │
     │      org in private.staff_org_ids() (string compare, no uuid cast); no  │
     │      UPDATE/DELETE policy.                                              │

     │ ---                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 3. Key design decisions (recommendation, why)                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D1 Migrations vs Prisma. Migrations are the source of truth;            │
     │ schema.prisma is generated. Needed for RLS, grants, triggers, the       │
     │ exclusion constraint and the bucket, none of which Prisma Migrate       │
     │ models. Sync: npm run db:sync = supabase db reset && prisma db pull &&  │
     │ prisma generate, never hand-edit schema.prisma, commit it after every   │
     │ migration change. db pull cannot express the EXCLUDE/CHECKs; that is    │
     │ fine (they live in SQL), and I will verify the pull is clean in Phase   │
     │ 2. Prisma is used only for server-side data access (as owner, bypassing │
     │ RLS).                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D2 Seed. scripts/seed.ts (run by tsx --env-file=.env.local) calls the   │
     │ same service functions the API uses, with an Actor object, not HTTP.    │
     │ Users via supabase.auth.admin.createUser({ email_confirm: true,         │
     │ password }) with the secret key; memberships/tenants/units via Prisma;  │
     │ leases through createLease; payments through recordPayment (manager ⇒   │
     │ approved, staff ⇒ pending) then approvePayment, so invoices,            │
     │ allocations and audit rows are exactly what the app produces. Slip PNG  │
     │ uploaded with upsert: true (db reset does not clear storage volumes).   │
     │ Refuses to run if orgs already exist (prints "run npx supabase db       │
     │ reset"), so a double run cannot create duplicates.                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D3 Auth helper. One getActor(req) in src/lib/auth.ts. If an             │
     │ Authorization: Bearer header exists, validate it with getUser(token)    │
     │ using a stateless client built from the publishable key, and a bad      │
     │ token is 401 with no cookie fallback. Otherwise use the @supabase/ssr   │
     │ cookie client's getUser(). getUser over getClaims because it checks     │
     │ with the Auth server (sees revoked users) and the local Auth is fast.   │
     │ Then role lookup: memberships by user_id ⇒ staff/manager + orgId; else  │
     │ tenants by user_id ⇒ tenant + tenantId + orgId; valid login with        │
     │ neither ⇒ 403. Fixed pipeline in every handler: authenticate (401) →    │
     │ load the record through a visibility-scoped finder (404; malformed UUID │
     │ is also 404) → role check (403) → body validation (400) → state check   │
     │ (409) → cap/balance (422). The finder scopes by org_id for              │
     │ staff/manager and by tenant_id for tenants, so cross-org and            │
     │ cross-tenant are indistinguishable from "missing". route() wrapper maps │
     │ HttpError to {error} JSON. Server Actions and server components call    │
     │ the same getActor.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D4 Concurrency and overlap. Lock the lease row. Every money-changing    │
     │ operation (record+cap, approve, reject, void, webhook allocation) runs  │
     │ in one prisma.$transaction, first statement SELECT id FROM leases WHERE │
     │ id = $1 FOR UPDATE, then re-reads the payment/bill status inside the    │
     │ lock and guards the transition. The cap sum, the approve re-check,      │
     │ void, and allocation therefore all serialize per lease, and "approve    │
     │ twice" allocates once (second waits, sees approved, returns 409).       │
     │ Webhook lock order is bill → lease; nothing locks lease → bill, so no   │
     │ deadlock. Business logic stays in TypeScript services (explainable line │
     │ by line) instead of PL/pgSQL functions, which would also be new rpc     │
     │ surface to lock down. Overlap: EXCLUDE USING gist (unit_id WITH =,      │
     │ daterange(start_date, end_date, '[]') WITH &&): the database rejects    │
     │ the second concurrent insert (SQLSTATE 23P01 ⇒ 409), no app-level lock  │
     │ needed. Backstops: UNIQUE(payment_id, invoice_id) on allocations.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D5 Webhook idempotency. Row lock plus unique index. Order: parse raw    │
     │ body with URLSearchParams (duplicate keys ⇒ treated as bad signature) → │
     │ verify HMAC (constant time; lengths checked first) ⇒ 401 → read id →    │
     │ tx: SELECT ... FROM gateway_bills WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE (none ⇒ 404) → │
     │ paid=true: amount mismatch ⇒ 422; paid ⇒ 200 no-op; else lock lease,    │
     │ insert the approved online payment (gateway_bill_id, received_at =      │
     │ paid_at parsed with its offset), allocate, set bill paid, audit →       │
     │ paid=false: only pending → failed. A second concurrent delivery blocks  │
     │ on the bill lock, then sees paid and returns 200.                       │
     │ UNIQUE(payments.gateway_bill_id) is the structural guarantee ("at most  │
     │ one payment, ever"); a 23505 is treated as a replay (200), never 500.   │
     │ Logging: only bill id + outcome codes; no body, no key.                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D6 Data API lockdown. Deny by default, grant narrowly, helpers off the  │
     │ API surface. REVOKE ALL on public tables from PUBLIC, anon,             │
     │ authenticated (service_role keeps its grants, per Q7), then GRANT       │
     │ SELECT to authenticated only on the 9 readable tables, plus             │
     │ column-level UPDATE (phone) on tenants. Defaults are neutralised by     │
     │ revoking after creation in the same migration (plus ALTER DEFAULT       │
     │ PRIVILEGES for the migration role). Policies TO authenticated, built on │
     │ private.* SECURITY DEFINER helpers (avoids recursive RLS).              │
     │ authenticated gets USAGE on schema private and EXECUTE on the helpers   │
     │ (RLS needs both); they are still not callable via /rest/v1/rpc because  │
     │ nothing lives in public and private is not in api.schemas. EXECUTE is   │
     │ revoked from PUBLIC and anon only. A probe test proves rpc calls to     │
     │ each private.* helper fail. Tenant phone update = column grant + RLS    │
     │ user_id = auth.uid() (full_name/email/user_id/org_id updates fail with  │
     │ permission denied). audit_log: triggers on UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE raise │
     │ for everyone (owner and service_role included); UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE  │
     │ also revoked from anon/authenticated; INSERT only by Prisma/owner.      │
     │ service_role keeps table privileges (Q7): the tests use the publishable │
     │ key + user tokens and the secret key never leaves the server, so the    │
     │ triggers are what make audit_log append-only for it.                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D7 Storage and cleanup. Bucket in a migration: private, 5 MB,           │
     │ jpeg/png/pdf. Policies: INSERT and SELECT for staff/managers whose org  │
     │ id equals the first path segment; no UPDATE/DELETE, no tenant or anon   │
     │ policy. Signed URLs (60 s) created with the user-scoped client, so the  │
     │ policy is a second enforcement layer. Upload happens first (client,     │
     │ user token), then POST .../payments carries slipPath. Server            │
     │ validation: path starts with <actor.orgId>/, no .., and a               │
     │ storage.objects row exists for the bucket (also re-checks size and      │
     │ mimetype). Cleanup: in the route's failure path, after the caller is    │
     │ authenticated as staff/manager, if slipPath is in the caller's own org  │
     │ folder and no payment references it, delete the object with the admin   │
     │ client. UNIQUE(slip_path) plus "only delete if unreferenced" means a    │
     │ failed request can never delete another payment's slip, and the loser   │
     │ of a concurrent same-slip race does not delete the winner's file.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D8 Time. Everything derives from three primitives in pure code:         │
     │ malaysiaDate(now) (billing.ts) for "today", parseOffsetInstant for      │
     │ incoming instants (rejects strings without an offset), SQL AT TIME ZONE │
     │ 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur' for month bucketing (report range = [first-of-from  │
     │ 00:00 MYT, first-of-(to+1) 00:00 MYT), zero months filled in TS from    │
     │ monthRange). UI: formatMyt is pure arithmetic with no Intl/locale, so   │
     │ server and client render identical strings; datetime-local text is      │
     │ combined with +08:00 rather than parsed by the browser; no new          │
     │ Date()/Date.now() in render paths. This also makes DECISIONS Q3 a short │
     │ list.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D9 Money in TS. DB bigint ⇒ Prisma BigInt; services do arithmetic in    │
     │ BigInt or in SQL (SUM); a single senToNumber() at the API boundary      │
     │ asserts Number.isSafeInteger. billing.ts keeps the contract's number    │
     │ fields (integers only) and uses BigInt for proration.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 4. Risks and fallbacks                                                  │

     │ ▎ 13; snippet 2 starts at SPEC.md 443; snippet 3 at SPEC.md 485.        │

     │                                                                         │
     │ late-fees/route.ts                                                      │
     │ 1. Critical L401: role read from user_metadata (user-editable) ⇒ any    │
     │    user escalates to manager. Fix: role from memberships.               │
     │ 2. Critical L406: no org/ownership check on leaseId; Prisma bypasses    │
     │    RLS ⇒ a manager of org B adds fees to org A's lease (IDOR). Fix:     │
     │    scope lookup by actor's org ⇒ 404.                                   │
     │ 3. High L415–434: not idempotent (second call adds the same fees        │
     │    again), no transaction (partial writes on failure), no lease lock    │
     │    (concurrent calls duplicate).                                        │
     │ 4. High L429/L438–440: reference: await nextReference() is resolved     │
     │    before any create runs (Prisma queries are lazy), so every invoice   │
     │    in the loop gets the same INV-n; also read-then-write race and       │
     │    parseInt of a null/other-format reference gives INV-NaN.             │
     │ 5. Medium L421: kind: 'late_fee' is outside the allowed invoice kinds   │
     │    (deposit/rent) and breaks the lease balance/cap model.               │
     │ 6. Medium L419–420: money via floats (Number(...)/Number(...) >= 0.995  │
     │    silently forgives up to 0.5%; Math.round(x*2/100)). Fix: BigInt      │
     │    integer math, explicit rule.                                         │
     │ 7. Low L401–403: unauthenticated returns 403, should be 401; L427 new   │
     │    Date(todayMyt) fine (UTC midnight) but only works because Prisma     │
     │    reads DATE as UTC.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ webhooks/gateway/route.ts                                               │
     │ 1. Critical L458: logs fields and the expected signature ⇒ anyone with  │
     │    log access can forge valid callbacks (and logs whole payload,        │
     │    forbidden).                                                          │
     │ 2. High L463–475: read-then-write race, no lock; two concurrent         │
     │    deliveries both see pending ⇒ double payment (or 500 on unique).     │
     │    Fix: FOR UPDATE on bill row in the transaction + unique              │
     │    gateway_bill_id.                                                     │
     │ 3. High L461: paid !== 'true' returns 200 before the bill lookup ⇒      │
     │    unknown id never 404, pending bill never becomes failed.             │
     │ 4. High L475: BigInt(fields.paid_amount) throws on junk (500) and is    │
     │    never compared to bill.amountSen ⇒ attacker or gateway bug settles a │
     │    bill with any amount (422 required).                                 │
     │ 5. Medium L476: receivedAt: new Date() instead of paid_at ⇒ wrong month │
     │    in collections.                                                      │
     │ 6. Medium L457: !== string compare is not constant-time; ! on a missing │
     │    env var gives a 500.                                                 │
     │ 7. Medium L467–481: allocation without locking the lease ⇒ races with   │
     │    approvals/other allocations.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ dashboard/actions.ts                                                    │
     │ 1. Critical L490: 'use server' export is a public endpoint; orgId comes │
     │    from the client, no authentication/authorization ⇒ any caller        │
     │    (including tenants/anon) reads any org's collections.                │
     │ 2. High L492–495: date_trunc('month', received_at) and ::date           │
     │    comparisons use the DB session timezone (UTC) ⇒ payments between     │
     │    00:00–08:00 MYT on the 1st land in the previous month, range edges   │
     │    shifted 8 h. Fix: AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur' on both.          │
     │ 3. Medium L497: months with no payments are omitted (brief requires     │
     │    zero rows); no validation of from/to (bad input ⇒ 500).              │
     │ 4. Looks suspicious but fine: the tagged-template $queryRaw is          │
     │    parameterised (no SQL injection); Number(r.total) is safe within the │
     │    safe-integer range (type annotation bigint is wrong, since SUM       │
     │    returns numeric, but the conversion still works).                    │

     Corrections to this plan (also logged in docs/notes.md for DECISIONS Q6):
     - A. D6 / Phase 2 migration 3. RLS policies call private.* helpers, so
       authenticated needs USAGE on schema private and EXECUTE on those
       functions, otherwise every RLS query fails with permission denied. They
       stay unreachable via /rest/v1/rpc because private is not in api.schemas.
       Revoke EXECUTE from PUBLIC and anon only (not from authenticated). Phase
       2 adds a probe test proving rpc calls to the private.* helpers fail.
     - B. Appendix A. The L### numbers are line numbers in SPEC.md. REVIEW.md
       must use line numbers within each snippet file (late-fees/route.ts,
       webhooks/gateway/route.ts, dashboard/actions.ts), counted from 1 per
       file. Fixed in Phase 7.

     0. Reality check (what I found, differs from the brief you pasted)

     Finding: Next.js 16.4.3 / React 19.3 / Tailwind 4 already scaffolded (not
     empty). Git already has "Initial commit from Create Next App"; CLAUDE.md +
     docs/ untracked. Branch is master, main branch is main.
     Consequence: Phase 0 does not scaffold. Branch name: Open Q3.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: supabase/ does not exist and no Supabase containers are running
     (only
     an exited youthful_bell). Images are already pulled (postgres 17.11,
     gotrue,
     storage, postgrest, kong, studio...).
     Consequence: Phase 0 runs supabase init + supabase start (fast, no pulls).
     Not
     "already running".
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: next.config.ts has cacheComponents: true + partialPrefetching: 
     true
     (docs read). With Cache Components, any page reading cookies() outside
     <Suspense> is a build error, and request-time Date/random in server
     components needs care.
     Consequence: Open Q2: I recommend removing both flags.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: .gitignore has .env*, which also hides .env.example (required
     deliverable).
     Consequence: Add !.env.example.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: prisma CLI latest tag = 8.0.0-rc.28, but @prisma/client latest =
     7.10.1. A plain npm i -D prisma pulls an RC that does not match the client.

     Plausible cause of the hang.
     Consequence: Pin all three to 7.10.1, install with --foreground-scripts.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: TZ=... is silently dropped by Git Bash (verified: process.env.TZ
     was
     undefined). PowerShell and a Node child process honour it (verified: KL
     gives
      offset -480, NY gives 300). This machine is UTC+2, so neither test zone is

     the local one.
     Consequence: TZ tests run through scripts/test-tz.mjs (spawns vitest with
     env.TZ set), never inline TZ= in bash.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Finding: Next 16 conventions (read in node_modules/next/dist/docs):
     proxy.ts
     replaces middleware.ts; params is a Promise; cookies() is async;
     RouteContext<'/path'> helper exists. Proxy is not an authorization layer.
     Consequence: Auth is checked in every handler. proxy.ts only refreshes the
     cookie session and does optimistic redirects.
     ────────────────────────────────────────

     1. Requirements checklist

     Legend: ⛔ = INSTANT FAIL. "Phase" = where it gets built/verified. Weights
     from "How We Assess" (total 100).

     ⛔ The 4 instant-fail rules (checked in every phase, re-verified in Phase 
     7)

     ID: ⛔ IF1
     Rule: Service-role/secret key never reaches the browser
     Guard: Only SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY (no NEXT_PUBLIC_), used only in import 
     'server-only' modules. After build: grep -r 
     "sb_secret|SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY" 
     .next/static must be empty.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: ⛔ IF2
     Rule: No table anon can read or write through the Data API
     Guard: All grants revoked from anon; no anon policies; probe test over
     every
     table/function.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: ⛔ IF3
     Rule: No floating-point money
     Guard: bigint sen in DB; BigInt in TS domain code; billing proration in
     BigInt.
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: ⛔ IF4
     Rule: A bill/invoice is never settled by the return page
     Guard: /payments/return is read-only; only the webhook route mutates
     gateway_bills/payments.

     S: Setup, seed, deliverables (gates everything)

     ID: S1
     Rule: Fresh clone works with exactly: npx supabase start, npx supabase db 
     reset, npm install, cp .env.example .env.local, npm run seed, npm run 
     build 
     && npm start
     Phase: 0, 3, CP1, 7
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S2
     Rule: Commit supabase/config.toml, supabase/migrations/, .env.example
     (every
     var, local values, comment on where each comes from). Never commit real
     .env*
     Phase: 0
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S3
     Rule: Env vars: NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
     NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY,
     SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY, DATABASE_URL, DIRECT_URL, GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY
     Phase: 0
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S4
     Rule: npm run seed on a freshly reset DB creates exactly the seed data,
     through
      our lease/payment code paths
     Phase: 2→4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S5
     Rule: Orgs Northwind Properties, Beta Realty. Accounts (one shared
     password):
     manager@northwind.test (manager), staff@northwind.test (staff),
     aisyah@northwind.test (tenant Aisyah Rahman), ben@northwind.test (Ben Lim),

     probe@northwind.test (Probe Tenant), manager@beta.test (manager),
     chen@beta.test (tenant Chen Wei)
     Phase: 2
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S6
     Rule: Units/leases: A-12-3 Aisyah 2026-01-31→2026-07-15 RM1,500.00 dep
     RM3,000.00; B-05-1 Ben 2026-01-01→2026-04-15 RM1,000.29 no deposit; C-01-7
     Probe 2026-02-28→2027-02-27 RM2,000.00 dep RM4,000.00; D-03-2 no lease;
     Beta
     KL-1 Chen 2026-03-15→2027-03-14 RM2,200.00 dep RM4,400.00
     Phase: 3
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S7
     Rule: Aisyah payments: cash 300000 sen received 2026-01-31 10:00 MYT by
     manager; bank_transfer 200000 sen received 2026-03-01 00:30 MYT by staff
     with
      slip file, then approved by manager
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S8
     Rule: gateway-sim.mjs saved at repo root, verbatim from brief
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: S9
     Rule: Deliverables at root: README.md (run, known issues, next steps, what 
     was 
     skipped), REVIEW.md, DECISIONS.md, ai-logs/ (/export of every AI session),
     prisma/schema.prisma, seed script, .env.example
     Phase: CP1, 7

     B: Billing, src/lib/billing.ts (15 pts; hidden ~50 cases under TZ=UTC, 
     Asia/Kuala_Lumpur, America/New_York)

     Exports (contract): InvoiceKind, ScheduledInvoice, generateSchedule,
     InvoiceStatus, invoiceStatus, parseRinggitToSen. Pure: no I/O, no
     Next/React/Supabase/Prisma imports.

     ID: B1
     Rule: generateSchedule throws on: non-real calendar date or not YYYY-MM-DD
     (2026-02-30, 2026-2-3, 2026-13-01), endDate < startDate, monthlyRentSen not

     positive integer, depositSen not non-negative integer
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B2
     Rule: depositSen > 0 ⇒ first invoice kind:'deposit', due startDate,
     periodIndex/periodStart/periodEnd = null. depositSen = 0 ⇒ none
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B3
     Rule: Anchor k = start + k calendar months from the start date (never from
     the
     previous anchor); clamp to month's last day (Jan 31 → Feb 28/29 → Mar 31 →
     Apr 30)
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B4
     Rule: Period k = anchor k … day before anchor k+1 (periodEnd inclusive),
     due on
     anchor k; one period per anchor on or before endDate
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B5
     Rule: Last period cut at endDate; if shorter than full:
     round_half_up(monthlyRentSen × daysUsed / daysInFullPeriod); all others
     cost
     monthlyRentSen. If endDate = last day of a full period ⇒ full price (Probe,

     Chen)
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B6
     Rule: Order: deposit, then rent by periodIndex. Aisyah example = 7 rows,
     last =
     77419; Ben's last = 50015 (50014.5 rounds up)
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B7
     Rule: invoiceStatus(inv, now) in order: paid if paidSen ≥ amountSen →
     overdue
     if Malaysia date of now > dueDate → partially_paid if paidSen > 0 → unpaid.

     Due 2026-03-01 turns overdue at 2026-03-01T16:00:00Z, not 1 ms earlier
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B8
     Rule: parseRinggitToSen: digits, optional comma thousands (groups of
     exactly
     3), optional 1–2 digit decimals, trims whitespace. "1,500.50"→150050,
     "1.15"→115, "7"→700. Throws on "", "-5", "1.234", "1e3", "RM 10", "1,23"
     (also "1.", ".5", unsafe-integer overflow)
     Phase: 1
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: B9
     Rule: No dependence on machine TZ (UTC-only math; never new 
     Date('YYYY-MM-DD')
     + local getters); suite green under all 3 TZs; used by the app (server and
     browser)
     Phase: 1

     P: Payments (15 pts)

     ID: P1
     Rule: Staff/manager record cash or bank_transfer with amountSen, method,
     receivedAt (ISO 8601 with offset). Bank transfer requires slip
     (JPEG/PNG/PDF
     ≤ 5 MB) in Storage; if recording fails after upload, the  file is not left 
     behind
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P2
     Rule: Manager-recorded ⇒ approved immediately (and allocated).
     Staff-recorded ⇒
     pending_verification
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P3
     Rule: Cap: new amount ≤ (Σ invoices − Σ allocations) − Σ
     pending_verification
     payments of that lease; future periods allowed within cap; else 422
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P4
     Rule: Approve (manager): only pending_verification (else 409); re-check
     amount
     ≤ remaining balance, else 422 and stays pending
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P5
     Rule: Reject (manager): only pending (else 409); reason ≥ 5 chars (else
     400)
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P6
     Rule: Void (manager): only approved (else 409); reason ≥ 5 chars (400);
     deletes
     that payment's allocations only
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P7
     Rule: Payments never deleted. audit_log row (actor_id, action, entity_id,
     details, created_at) for every approval, rejection, void and lease creation
     Phase: 3, 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P8
     Rule: Allocation on approval: unpaid invoices ordered by due_date, then
     deposit
     before rent, then period_index; fill each fully (incl. not-yet-due) before
     the next; leftover → payments.unapplied_sen (only online can have any).
     paidSen = Σ allocations; status computed with invoiceStatus, never stored
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P9
     Rule: Concurrency: two staff record RM800 + RM700 on RM1,000 remaining ⇒
     exactly one accepted; double approve at once ⇒ allocated once
     Phase: 4
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: P10
     Rule: GET /api/reports/collections?from=YYYY-MM&to=YYYY-MM: Σ approved
     payments
     per month of received_at in MYT, every month in range incl. zeros, ordered.

     Seed: Northwind 2026-01..03 =
     [{2026-01,300000},{2026-02,0},{2026-03,200000}]
      (the 2026-03-01 00:30 MYT payment is 2026-02-28T16:30Z, the trap)
     Phase: 4

     W: Online payments & webhook (15 pts)

     ID: W1
     Rule: POST /api/leases/:leaseId/gateway-bills (the lease's tenant only):
     amount
     = Σ unpaid of invoices with due_date ≤ today (MYT); nothing due ⇒ 422; 201 
     { 
     billId, amountSen }; gateway_bills.id unguessable (bill_ + 122-bit random)
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: ⛔ W2
     Rule: /payments/return?bill_id=...&paid=true only displays the DB status,
     changes nothing (IF4)
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W3
     Rule: POST /api/webhooks/gateway, application/x-www-form-urlencoded; extra
     fields allowed and included in the signature
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W4
     Rule: Signature: all fields except x_signature, sort keys ascending by char

     code, key+value joined with |, HMAC-SHA256 hex lowercase with
     GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY, constant-time compare. Vector: key
     test_x_signature_key ⇒
     06994fab2d674e7829144eaa2204631716d3c099b503d38945e92b6296531641
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W5
     Rule: Table: sig missing/wrong ⇒ 401 none; unknown bill ⇒ 404; paid=true &
     paid_amount ≠ bill amount ⇒ 422; paid=true & pending/failed ⇒ 200, bill
     paid
     + ONE approved online payment, received_at = paid_at, allocated; paid=true
     &
     already paid ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & pending ⇒ 200, bill failed;
     paid=false
      & failed ⇒ 200 no-op; paid=false & already paid ⇒ 200 no-op (paid never
     goes
      back)
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W6
     Rule: Duplicate/concurrent deliveries ⇒ at most one payment ever (UNIQUE 
     payments.gateway_bill_id + bill row lock)
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W7
     Rule: Never log the secret or whole payloads
     Phase: 5
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     ID: W8
     Rule: node gateway-sim.mjs <bill_id> <amount_sen> [true|false] works end to
     end
     Phase: 5

     H: HTTP API contract (JSON; sen integers; dates YYYY-MM-DD; instants ISO 
     8601 with offset; errors { "error": "message" }; every endpoint can also 
     return 401/403/404)

     Endpoint: POST /api/leases body
     {unitId,tenantId,startDate,endDate,monthlyRentSen,depositSen}
     Who: manager
     Success: 201 {id}
     Other: 400, 409 overlap
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: GET /api/leases/:leaseId
     Who: staff, manager, lease's tenant
     Success: 200
     {lease:{id,unitId,tenantId,startDate,endDate,monthlyRentSen,depositSen},  
     invoices:[{id,kind,periodIndex,periodStart,periodEnd,dueDate,amountSen,paid
     Sen,status}]
       (allocation order), 
     payments:[{id,amountSen,unappliedSen,method,status,receivedAt}]}
     Other:
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/leases/:leaseId/payments body
     {amountSen,method:'cash'|'bank_transfer',receivedAt,slipPath?}
     Who: staff, manager
     Success: 201 {id,status}
     Other: 400, 422 over cap
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/payments/:paymentId/approve
     Who: manager
     Success: 200 {status}
     Other: 409 not pending, 422 over balance
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/payments/:paymentId/reject body {reason}
     Who: manager
     Success: 200 {status}
     Other: 400 missing reason, 409 not pending
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/payments/:paymentId/void body {reason}
     Who: manager
     Success: 200 {status}
     Other: 400 missing reason, 409 not approved
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/leases/:leaseId/gateway-bills
     Who: lease's tenant
     Success: 201 {billId,amountSen}
     Other: 422 nothing due
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: GET /api/reports/collections?from&to
     Who: staff, manager
     Success: 200 [{month,collectedSen}]
     Other: 400 invalid range
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Endpoint: POST /api/webhooks/gateway
     Who: gateway
     Success: see W5
     Other:

     DB: Database (migrations are the source of truth)

     Rule: All objects in supabase/migrations/ (tables, constraints, RLS,

     Phase: 2

     leases(id,org_id,unit_id,tenant_id,start_date,end_date,monthly_rent_sen,dep
     osit_sen),
     invoices(id,org_id,lease_id,kind,period_index,period_start,period_end,due_d
     ate,amount_sen),
     payments(id,org_id,lease_id,amount_sen,unapplied_sen,method,status,received
     _at,slip_path,gateway_bill_id),

     Phase: 2

     T: Timezones (10 pts, cross-cutting)

     T1 overdue boundary in MYT (B7) · T2 collections bucketed by MYT month
     (P10) · T3 paid_at +0800 → received_at instant (W5) · T4 every input
     (datetime for payments) interpreted as MYT whatever the browser TZ · T5
     every display in MYT whatever the browser TZ, no hydration warnings · T6
     billing identical under 3 TZs (B9).

     U: UI & code quality (10 pts)

     shadcn/ui + Tailwind throughout · toasts on every action (success and
     error) · loading states (skeletons), no blank screens · helpful
     empty/invalid-input messages · TS strict, no any.
     - U1 Staff/manager dashboard: overdue-invoice count + collections report,
       last 6 months
     - U2 Leases list; lease page: invoices (period, due, amount, paid, status)
       + payments (slip link for bank transfers via signed URL)
     - U3 Record payment: ringgit text → parseRinggitToSen; datetime in MYT;
       slip upload for bank transfer
     - U4 Manager: approve / reject / void (confirm step + reason)
     - U5 Tenant: lease, invoices with statuses, payments, amount due now, Pay
       now (shows bill id + amount), /payments/return, edit phone
     - U6 npm run build + tests green before every commit

     Part B, C, Bonus

     - RV (5 pts) REVIEW.md: per problem line numbers, concrete scenario,
       severity, fix, ordered most→least severe; no false positives (may state
       "looks suspicious but correct"). Prep notes in Appendix A.
     - DC (5 pts) DECISIONS.md: 6 answers ≤ 150 words each with repo file:line
       pointers. Written after code freeze.
     - Bonus (only if done well, skip unless ahead): create-lease form with live
       schedule preview; realtime; unit tests for billing + allocation
       (billing/allocation tests are in the core plan anyway); URL-synced
       search/pagination.

     2. Phased plan

     Build budget 3h05m + 30m write-ups = 3h35m. Commit trailer on every commit:
     Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>.

     ┌────────────────────────────────────────────┬─────┬──────────────────────┐
     │                   Phase                    │ Box │       Elapsed        │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 0 Setup                                    │ 15m │ 0:00–0:15            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 1 Billing + TZ tests                       │ 20m │ 0:15–0:35            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 2 Schema + access control (DB side) + seed │ 35m │ 0:35–1:10            │
     │  stage 1                                   │     │                      │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 3 Auth helper + leases API + seed stage 2  │ 15m │ 1:10–1:25            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ ★ CP1 Safe submission #1                   │ 10m │ 1:25–1:35            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 4 Payments + allocation + report + seed    │ 35m │ 1:35–2:10            │
     │ stage 3                                    │     │                      │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 5 Gateway bills + webhook + return page    │ 20m │ 2:10–2:30            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ ★ CP2 tag + push                           │ 2m  │ 2:30                 │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 6 UI                                       │ 35m │ 2:30–3:05            │
     ├────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ 7 REVIEW, DECISIONS, README, ai-logs,      │ 30m │ 3:05–3:35 (code      │
     │ final fresh-clone check, submit            │     │ freeze at 3:05)      │
     └────────────────────────────────────────────┴─────┴──────────────────────┘

     Overrun rule: if a phase runs >10 min over, apply the cut list (Section 4)
     and move on.
     Shared verify helper (built in Phase 3): node scripts/token.mjs <email>
     prints a Supabase access token (POST /auth/v1/token?grant_type=password),
     used as -H "Authorization: Bearer $T" in curl.

     Phase 0: Setup (15m)

     - Do
       a. docker ps; npx supabase init; npx supabase start; npx supabase status 
          -o env (copy keys). In supabase/config.toml set [api] schemas = 
          ["public"] (drops graphql_public from the Data API surface); leave
          signup default.
       b. Fix .gitignore (!.env.example). Write .env.example + .env.local (local
          URL, publishable key, secret key, DATABASE_URL=DIRECT_URL=postgresql:
          //postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres,
          GATEWAY_X_SIGNATURE_KEY=test_x_signature_key) with a comment per var
          saying which supabase status field it comes from.

          -D vitest tsx.

          @prisma/adapter-pg@7.10.1 --foreground-scripts then npm i -D 
          prisma@7.10.1 --foreground-scripts --loglevel=http. If no progress for
          3 min: Ctrl+C, retry with --ignore-scripts, then npx prisma -v with
          DEBUG=* to see which download stalls (check npm config get registry,
          antivirus, proxy). Confirm the Prisma 7 API from the installed package
          before using it (config file prisma.config.ts, driver adapter,
          generator output path); my recollection may be stale.
       e. npx shadcn@latest init -d only (components added in Phase 6). If the
          registry call stalls, hand-write components.json and defer.
       f. package.json scripts: test, test:tz, test:int, typecheck, seed,
          db:reset, db:pull, postinstall (prisma generate).
       g. (If Open Q2 = remove) drop cacheComponents/partialPrefetching from
          next.config.ts.
     - Files: supabase/config.toml, .env.example, .gitignore, package.json,
       prisma/ + prisma.config.ts, components.json, vitest.config.ts,
       docs/PLAN.md, docs/notes.md (running log of AI mistakes for DECISIONS
       Q6).

          leases(tenant_id,org_id)→tenants(id,org_id); EXCLUDE USING gist

          payments.created_by/decided_by/decided_at/decision_reason,

          statement trigger BEFORE TRUNCATE raising for every role incl.
          service_role and the owner (D10); additionally revoke
          UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE from anon/authenticated only.
       c. *_security.sql: schema private (not in api.schemas) holding SECURITY 
          DEFINER helpers with set search_path = '' (staff_org_ids(),
          manager_org_ids(), my_tenant_ids(), my_lease_ids()); ENABLE ROW LEVEL 
          SECURITY on all 10 tables; REVOKE ALL ... FROM PUBLIC, anon, 
          authenticated on public tables (not service_role, per Q7) and REVOKE 
          EXECUTE ... FROM PUBLIC, anon on functions; GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA 
          private + GRANT EXECUTE on the private.* helpers TO authenticated
          (required for RLS to evaluate); then GRANT SELECT to authenticated per
          D1–D6 and GRANT UPDATE (phone) ON tenants + RLS update policy user_id
          = auth.uid(); policies are TO authenticated only; no write policies
          anywhere else.
       d. *_storage.sql: bucket payment-slips (private, file_size_limit 5242880,
          allowed_mime_types jpeg/png/pdf); storage.objects policies for
          authenticated: INSERT and SELECT only when bucket_id='payment-slips'
          and the first folder segment equals an org in private.staff_org_ids()
          (string compare, no uuid cast); no UPDATE/DELETE policy.
     - Then: npm run db:sync (= supabase db reset → prisma db pull → prisma
